# META
~~~ini
description=Explicit action body parameters preserve loop-body names, typing, ownership and multiplicity
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package LoopBodies {
    action def Charge;
    action def Drive {
        action repeatCharge loop action <chg> charging : Charge[1] {
            action step;
        } until true;
        action keepCharging while true action whileCharge : Charge {
            action step;
        }
        action visit for element in (1, 2) action : Charge {
            action step;
        }
        action wait loop action {
            action step;
        } until true;
    }
}
~~~
